The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>48&minus;1 =</span> <strong>47</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>144&minus;1 =</span> <strong>143</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>84&minus;1 =</span> <strong>83</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>180&minus;1 =</span> <strong>179</strong>	Incorrect

The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the degrees of freedom for this chi-square (&chi;<sup>2</sup>) test? The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>156&minus;1 =</span> <strong>155</strong>	Incorrect

The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the degrees of freedom for this chi-square (&chi;<sup>2</sup>) test? The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>144&minus;1 =</span> <strong>143</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>60&minus;1 =</span> <strong>59</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>168&minus;1 =</span> <strong>167</strong>	Incorrect

The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the degrees of freedom for this chi-square (&chi;<sup>2</sup>) test? The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>42&minus;1 =</span> <strong>41</strong>	Incorrect

The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the degrees of freedom for this chi-square (&chi;<sup>2</sup>) test? The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>162&minus;1 =</span> <strong>161</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>30&minus;1 =</span> <strong>29</strong>	Incorrect

The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the degrees of freedom for this chi-square (&chi;<sup>2</sup>) test? The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>60&minus;1 =</span> <strong>59</strong>	Incorrect

The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the degrees of freedom for this chi-square (&chi;<sup>2</sup>) test? The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>60&minus;1 =</span> <strong>59</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>54&minus;1 =</span> <strong>53</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>144&minus;1 =</span> <strong>143</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>84&minus;1 =</span> <strong>83</strong>	Incorrect

The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability 1/6 on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the degrees of freedom for this chi-square (&chi;<sup>2</sup>) test? The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>108&minus;1 =</span> <strong>107</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>120&minus;1 =</span> <strong>119</strong>	Incorrect

The arithmetic is shown; no calculator is needed.</p>	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;2 =</span> <strong>4</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;1 =</span> <strong>5</strong>	Correct	<span style='font-size:85%;'>6&minus;0 =</span> <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style='font-size:85%;'>66&minus;1 =</span> <strong>65</strong>	Incorrect
